Based on my thorough investigation of the task feature codebase, here is my comprehensive report:

## Type Search Results

### 1. **ScheduleDriver** - DOES NOT EXIST in current codebase
- Found only in design documents as a planned type
- Location: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/codestable/features/rewrite-task/task-real-design.md`
- **Status**: Not implemented, marked as future work in design-code-alignment-audit.md

### 2. **StepRepeat** - DOES NOT EXIST in current codebase  
- Found only in design documents as a planned type
- Location: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/codestable/features/rewrite-task/task-real-design.md`
- **Status**: Not implemented, marked as future work in design-code-alignment-audit.md

### 3. **FieldVariation** - DOES NOT EXIST in current codebase
- Found only in design documents as a planned type
- Location: `/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/codestable/features/rewrite-task/task-real-design.md`
- **Status**: Not implemented, marked as future work in design-code-alignment-audit.md

### 4. **Current TaskStep Type Structure**

The current implementation uses **TaskStepDefinition** as a union type:

```typescript
export type TaskStepDefinition =
  | SendStepDefinition
  | WaitConditionStepDefinition
  | DelayStepDefinition;
```

Each specific step type has its own configuration interface:
- **SendStepDefinition**: `{ id, name?, kind: 'send', sendConfig: SendStepConfig }`
- **WaitConditionStepDefinition**: `{ id, name?, kind: 'wait-condition', waitConfig: WaitConditionStepConfig }`
- **DelayStepDefinition**: `{ id, name?, kind: 'delay', delayConfig: DelayStepConfig }`

### 5. **Schedule-Related Types**

Current implementation uses **TaskSchedulingMode** instead of ScheduleDriver:

```typescript
export const TASK_SCHEDULING_MODES = ['timed', 'trigger', 'sequence'] as const;
export type TaskSchedulingMode = (typeof TASK_SCHEDULING_MODES)[number];
```

Related scheduling types exist as individual fields on TaskDefinition:
- `intervalMs?: number`
- `triggerCondition?: WaitCondition` 
- `delayBeforeStartMs?: number`
- `cooldownMs?: number`
- `TaskTriggerSource` - enum-like type for trigger sources

### 6. **Current TaskDefinition Type**

```typescript
export interface TaskDefinition {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly schedulingMode: TaskSchedulingMode;  // Uses old mode system
  readonly triggerSource: TaskTriggerSource;
  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
  readonly targetId?: string;
  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
  readonly intervalMs?: number;
  readonly delayBeforeStartMs?: number;
  readonly triggerCondition?: WaitCondition;
  readonly cooldownMs?: number;
}
```

### 7. **Complete Type Inventory from types.ts**

**Core Constants & Enums:**
- `TASK_STEP_KINDS` - ['send', 'wait-condition', 'delay']
- `COMPARISON_OPERATORS` - ['eq', 'neq', 'gt', 'lt', 'gte', 'lte', 'contains', 'change', 'any']
- `TASK_TRIGGER_SOURCES` - ['user-ui', 'timer', 'receive-trigger', 'scoe-command', 'northbound-command']
- `TASK_SCHEDULING_MODES` - ['timed', 'trigger', 'sequence']
- `TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS` - ['retry', 'skip-step', 'stop', 'pause']
- `TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES` - ['created', 'running', 'paused', 'stopped', 'completed', 'failed']

**Core Interfaces:**
- `WaitCondition` - condition matching structure
- `ConditionTerm` - extends WaitCondition with logicOperator
- `ConditionMatchInput` - input for condition evaluation
- `SendStepConfig`, `WaitConditionStepConfig`, `DelayStepConfig` - step-specific configs
- `SendStepDefinition`, `WaitConditionStepDefinition`, `DelayStepDefinition` - step definitions
- `TaskStepDefinition` - union of all step types
- `TaskDefinition` - main task definition
- `TaskErrorPolicy` - error handling configuration
- `TaskStopCondition` - stopping conditions (maxDurationMs, maxIterations)
- `SendStepResult`, `WaitConditionStepResult`, `DelayStepResult` - execution results
- `TaskStepResult` - extends result payloads with metadata
- `TaskInstanceState` - runtime instance state
- `TaskProgress` - execution progress tracking
- `TaskExecutionSummary` - completion summary

**Additional Types from Other Files:**
- `SerializedTaskDefinition` - serialization wrapper
- `TaskValidationIssue` - validation error structure
- `TaskStateSnapshot`, `TaskStatisticsSnapshot` - state management
- `TaskService`, `TaskReader` - service interfaces
- `ReceiveEventSource`, `SendServiceProvider` - adapter ports
- `TaskUiSnapshot` - UI-specific snapshot

### **Key Findings:**

The codebase shows a clear evolutionary gap between the current implementation and the planned design. The design documents outline a more sophisticated system with ScheduleDriver, FieldVariation, and StepRepeat types, but these are not yet implemented. The current system uses simpler, flatter types with individual scheduling parameters instead of the discriminated union approach planned for the future.

# Comprehensive Report: Task-Related Documents Analysis

## Document 1: Design-Code Alignment Audit (`design-code-alignment-audit.md`)

### Main Conclusion/Recommendation (§2.4 & §5)
The audit reveals a **critical split**: task-real's core type system restructuring has NOT been implemented, but readiness patches (T-R1 through T-R5) were completed on the old type system. The recommendation allows task UI pages to proceed based on existing old types, while task-real restructuring can be scheduled independently.

### Specific Changes Proposed (§2.4 - Task Related Findings)

**Already Implemented (Readiness Patches):**
- `validateTaskDefinition` (55 lines) ✓
- Builder functions: `createSendStep`, `createDelayStep`, `createWaitConditionStep` (75 lines) ✓
- Serialization: `serializeTaskDefinition`/`deserializeTaskDefinition` (28 lines) ✓
- Control methods: `retryTask`, `stopAll` ✓
- Selector: `selectActiveInstances` ✓

**NOT Implemented (16 Core Items):**
1. `ScheduleDriver` discriminated union - to replace `TaskSchedulingMode`
2. `FieldVariation` type - new concept
3. `StepRepeat` type - new concept  
4. `TaskDefinition.schedule: ScheduleDriver` - replacing `schedulingMode` + flat fields
5. `TaskStepDefinition.config` unified field naming - still using `sendConfig`/`waitConfig`/`delayConfig`
6. `SendStepConfig.userFieldValues` - still using `fieldValues`
7. `SendStepConfig.targetId` required - still optional
8. `TaskStopCondition.exitCondition` - new feature
9. Unified `runTask` loop - still using switch to runTimedLoop/runTriggerLoop/runSequenceLoop
10. `ScheduleDriverAdapter` + `createDriver` - not implemented
11. `StopGuard` + `createStopGuard` - not implemented
12. `executeRepeatableSend` - not implemented
13. `evaluateCondition` signature change - still using old `(WaitCondition, ConditionMatchInput)`
14. `ConditionRegistry` AND/OR upgrade - still using single-condition interface
15. `CreateTaskServiceOptions.timerService` - missing
16. `CreateTaskServiceOptions.fieldValueProvider` - missing

### Blocker vs Nice-to-Have (§5)

**BLOCKER (Must implement - 5 items, large工作量):**
- **B1:** ScheduleDriver + TaskDefinition type restructuring (`task/core/types.ts`)
- **B2:** Unified runTask engine + ScheduleDriverAdapter (`task/services/task-iteration-loops.ts`)
- **B3:** evaluateCondition signature change (`task/core/condition-matcher.ts`)
- **B4:** ConditionRegistry upgrade to condition groups (`task/services/condition-registry.ts`)

- **H2:** Force readonly return values on task selectors

- **Pending:** Entire task-real core restructuring (all 16 items above)
- **Key Insight:** After design document terminology fixes, task pages can proceed with existing old type system while task-real restructuring happens in parallel

## Document 2: Task-Real Brainstorm (`task-real-brainstorm.md`)

### Main Conclusion/Recommendation
**Task-real is a unified execution engine** that receives TaskDefinition (step sequence + scheduling configuration), executes by strategy, and produces TaskInstanceCompletion events. The core design choice: **ScheduleDriver × stopCondition × step.repeat three-layer control** replaces the old three-mode enumeration.

### Specific Changes Proposed

**Core Design Decisions (9 decisions):**
1. **Remove IterationControl**, replace with stopCondition + step.repeat
2. **step.repeat only on send steps** (wait-condition already repeats, delay repetition is meaningless)
3. **OR combination retained** (old system has complete type definitions, runtime logic, and UI entry for AND/OR)
4. **No correlate** (old SCOE parameter association is SCOE adapter internal logic)
5. **No FieldValueSpec.expression** (expression evaluation belongs to send feature)
6. **fieldVariations automatically determines iterations**
7. **Single-frame sending uses same engine** (UI simplifies, constructs minimal TaskDefinition behind)
8. **SCOE maintains "translate to TaskDefinition" single responsibility**
9. **TimerService in platform facade**

**Old Mode Mapping:**
| Old Mode | New Expression |
|----------|----------------|
| Sequential send | `schedule: immediate`, no stopCondition, no repeat |
| Timed fixed N times | `schedule: timer(intervalMs)` + `stopCondition: { maxIterations: N }` |
| Timed infinite | `schedule: timer(intervalMs)`, no stopCondition |
| Conditional trigger | `schedule: event(conditions)` |
| Send A until X, then send B | `schedule: immediate`, step A `repeat.until: X`, step B no repeat |
| Timed send entire sequence until Y | `schedule: timer` + `stopCondition: { exitCondition: Y }` |
| File line-by-line send | `fieldVariations` auto maxIterations + `schedule: timer` |
| Single-frame send | `schedule: immediate`, one send step, no repeat |

### Blocker vs Nice-to-Have
- **BLOCKER:** All three-layer control implementation (ScheduleDriver + stopCondition + step.repeat)
- **BLOCKER:** Type system restructuring (TaskDefinition, ConditionTerm, FieldVariation, StepRepeat)
- **BLOCKER:** Unified runTask engine replacing three separate loops
- **Nice-to-have:** TimerService platform implementation (can use standard setTimeout initially)

### Implementation Status
- **Complete:** Full implementation skeleton exists in `rewrite/src/features/task/` (type system, service contracts, three-layer scheduling loop, polymorphic step execution, error policy engine, condition matching + O(1) index, adapter ports, state + 6 selectors)
- **Remaining:** Type restructuring (TaskSchedulingMode → ScheduleDriver + stopCondition), ConditionRegistry upgrade (single condition → AND/OR), new step.repeat capability (estimated 6-8 files, 300+ lines of existing code to modify)

---

## Document 3: Task-Real Design (`task-real-design.md`)

### Main Conclusion/Recommendation
Task-real will unify three independent scheduling loops (timed/trigger/sequence) into **ScheduleDriver discriminated union + stopCondition + step.repeat three-layer control**. Major changes: type restructuring, ConditionRegistry upgrade to AND/OR combination matching, send step adds repeat capability, `buildSendRequest` aligns with actual `SendRequest` API.

### Specific Changes Proposed

**Type System (§1):**
- **New types:** ScheduleDriver, ConditionTerm (with logicOperator), FieldVariation, StepRepeat, WaitConditionConfig
- **Major changes:** TaskDefinition (field reorganization), SendStepConfig (field changes), TaskStopCondition (add exitCondition), ConditionMatchInput (structure change)
- **Remove:** TaskSchedulingMode, WaitCondition, TaskTriggerSource, related constants
- **Invariant types:** TaskStepKind, ComparisonOperator, TaskErrorPolicy, lifecycle types, result types

**Loop Engine (§2):**
- Unified `runTask` replacing runTimedLoop/runTriggerLoop/runSequenceLoop
- Three ScheduleDriver implementations: immediate, timer, event
- StopGuard with maxIterations/maxDurationMs/exitCondition
- executeRepeatableSend for repeat capability
- fieldVariations injection in buildSendRequest

**ConditionRegistry Upgrade (§3):**
- From single-condition registration to condition group registration
- `evaluateConditionGroup` with AND/OR combination and short-circuit evaluation
- frameId indexing, sourceId filtering
- `ConditionMatchInput` changes from single field to multiple fields

**Step Executors (§4):**
- buildSendRequest alignment with SendRequest API (userFieldValues, variables, context)
- wait-condition step uses condition group registration
- intervalAfterMs handling in executeSteps loop

**TimerService Adapter (§5):**
- Interface defined in adapters/ports.ts
- Implementation in platform/ (Web Worker, doesn't block task-real)
- Optional injection, current code continues using standard setTimeout

### Blocker vs Nice-to-Have
- **BLOCKER:** All 5 MAJOR files (types.ts, condition-matcher.ts, task-iteration-loops.ts, condition-registry.ts, task-step-executors.ts)
- **BLOCKER:** All 13 MINOR files (progress.ts, clone.ts, task-service.ts, etc.)
- **Nice-to-have:** stepResults upper limit strategy (keep recent N entries, default 100)
- **Nice-to-have:** TimerService platform implementation (use standard setTimeout initially)

### Implementation Status

**Migration Plan (§7):**
- **26 files total** across core/, services/, adapters/, state/, selectors/, fixtures/, __tests__/
- **5 MAJOR files:** types.ts, condition-matcher.ts, task-iteration-loops.ts, condition-registry.ts, task-step-executors.ts
- **13 MINOR files:** Type following changes, small adaptations
- **7 FOLLOW files:** Index exports only
- **1 NO CHANGE:** lifecycle.ts

**Test Strategy (§7.3):**
- Unit tests for evaluateConditionGroup (AND, OR, mixed, short-circuit, empty, single conditions)
- ConditionRegistry group registration tests
- Unified runTask tests for timer/event/immediate
- repeat sending, fieldVariations injection, exitCondition tests
- stepResults upper limit tests
- All existing 70+ tests must be adapted and pass

**Checklist (§9):**
- Comprehensive checklist covering type system, loop engine, ConditionRegistry, step executors, adapters, tests, and build verification

---

## Cross-Document Synthesis

### What Has Been Implemented
1. **Readiness patches (Document 1):** validation, builders, serialization, control methods, selectors on OLD type system
2. **Skeleton exists (Document 2):** Full implementation skeleton in `rewrite/src/features/task/`
3. **Type system foundation:** Some types exist but need restructuring

### What Remains (BLOCKER items common to all documents)
1. **ScheduleDriver discriminated union** implementation
2. **Type system restructuring** (16 items listed in Document 1, §2.4)
3. **Unified runTask engine** replacing three separate loops
4. **ConditionRegistry upgrade** to AND/OR combination matching
5. **step.repeat capability** implementation
6. **buildSendRequest alignment** with SendRequest API
7. **TimerService integration** (interface defined, implementation pending)

### Implementation Strategy
- **Task pages can proceed** with existing old type system after design document terminology fixes
- **Task-real restructuring** can happen independently in parallel
- **Risk mitigation:** Clear separation between UI layer (can work with old types) and core engine restructuring (new types)

### Key Architectural Insights
1. **Three-layer control model** is settled: ScheduleDriver × stopCondition × step.repeat
2. **SCOE maintains single responsibility:** translate to TaskDefinition only
3. **Platform boundary respected:** TimerService in platform facade, not in task core
4. **Cross-feature coordination clear:** task → send via SendServiceProvider, task → receive via ReceiveEventSource adapter

### Critical Path Forward
1. Implement 5 MAJOR files (types, condition-matcher, iteration-loops, condition-registry, step-executors)
2. Adapt 13 MINOR files for type following
3. Ensure all 70+ existing tests pass with new implementation
4. Add new tests for AND/OR conditions, repeat, fieldVariations, exitCondition
5. Clean up residual files (H1 priority item)

## Analysis of UI Design Documents: Task-Real Types and Concepts Reference

Based on my thorough review of both documents, here are the findings:

## **Document 1: pages-ta[REDACTED_SK].md**

### **References to Schedule Types**
- **scheduleKind**: 0 direct references
- **ScheduleDriver**: Multiple references throughout (lines 63, 421, 435, 455, 469)
- **schedulingMode**: 0 direct references  
- **TaskSchedulingMode**: 0 direct references

### **References to Step Configuration**
- **step.repeat**: 0 direct references
- **StepRepeat**: 0 direct references
- **fieldVariations**: Referenced as "fieldVariations" (line 63, 352)
- **FieldVariation**: Referenced as "FieldVariation" (line 70, 352)

### **References to Stop/Exit Conditions**
- **stopCondition**: Referenced as "stopCondition" (line 63, 352)
- **exitCondition**: Referenced as "exitCondition" (line 352)

### **References to Condition Groups and Logic**
- **Condition groups**: Referenced through "ConditionTerm" (lines 68, 344)
- **AND/OR logic**: Referenced as "logicOperator" (line 68, 344)

### **Types/Interfaces Assumed**
The document assumes these types exist (from lines 63-71):
- `TaskDefinition`: { id, name, steps: TaskStepDefinition[], schedule: ScheduleDriver, stopCondition?, fieldVariations?, errorPolicy }
- `TaskStepDefinition`: SendStepDefinition | WaitConditionStepDefinition | DelayStepDefinition
- `ScheduleDriver`: { kind: 'immediate' } | { kind: 'timer', intervalMs } | { kind: 'event', conditions, cooldownMs? }
- `ConditionTerm`: { frameId, fieldId, operator, threshold, sourceId?, logicOperator? }
- `FieldVariation`: { fieldId, values[] }
- `TaskErrorPolicy`: { onFailure: TaskErrorAction, retryCount?, retryDelayMs? }

### **Type System Alignment**
**Written against NEW (design doc) type system** - The document explicitly references the current task feature types from the codebase, including ScheduleDriver with immediate/timer/event kinds (not the old type system).

### **Types/Interfaces Assumed**

### **Type System Alignment**
**Written against NEW (design doc) type system** - The design document creates new UI-specific types (like SendFrameInstance) and assumes the existence of updated task system types, explicitly avoiding the old system's types. Line 576 confirms: "TaskSchedulingMode 只有 timed/trigger/sequence"

---

## **Key Findings Summary**

1. **Schedule Terminology Mismatch**: The brainstorm doc uses `ScheduleDriver` (from core types), while the design doc uses `schedulingMode` (UI-facing abstraction)

2. **Step Repeat**: Only mentioned in design doc as "repeat 配置" - not formalized as a specific type

3. **Condition Logic**: Both documents assume condition-based logic but use different abstractions:
   - Brainstorm: ConditionTerm with logicOperator
   - Design: UI-level "条件编辑器" without explicit AND/OR type references

4. **Type System Status**: Both documents are aligned with the **NEW type system**, not the legacy system

5. **Missing Formal Types**: The design doc introduces UI-specific types (SendFrameInstance) that don't exist in the core layer

6. **Stop Conditions**: Both documents reference stopCondition/exitCondition but don't detail their structure

Both documents represent a transition from the OLD task system (embedded in send page dialogs) to a NEW independent task management page (/tasks) with its own type system and UI abstractions.

## Interface Contract Analysis: Task Feature Dependencies

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here are the actual interface contracts that the task feature currently consumes from its dependencies:

### 1. **Task Feature Adapter Ports** (`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/task/adapters/ports.ts`)

The task feature defines three adapter ports:

```typescript
// 1. Send Service Provider - for executing send operations
interface SendServiceProvider {
  execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>;
}

// 2. Receive Event Source - for subscribing to receive events
interface ReceiveEventSource {
  subscribe(handler: (input: ConditionMatchInput) => void): () => void;
}

// 3. Frame Reader - for reading frame definitions
interface FrameReader {
  getFrameAsset(id: string): ReadonlyFrameAsset | undefined;
}
```

### 2. **Send Feature Consumption** (`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/send/`)

**Types consumed:**
- `SendRequest` - Used in `task-step-executors.ts` to build send requests from task steps
- `SendResult` - Used to capture send operation results in task step results

**Current SendRequest interface:**
```typescript
interface SendRequest {
  readonly frameId: string;
  readonly fieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>;
  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>;
  readonly targetId: string;
  readonly options?: SendOptions;
  readonly context: SendContext;  // Contains source, taskId?, stepIndex?
}
```

**Usage in task feature:**
- `buildSendRequest()` function in `task-step-executors.ts` constructs SendRequest from task step definitions
- Includes `context: { source: 'task', taskId: definition.id, stepIndex }` for tracking
- Send results are stored in `TaskStepResult` for progress tracking

### 3. **Connection Feature Consumption** (`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/connection/`)

**Types consumed:**
- `TransportTargetSnapshot` - Indirectly used through send feature's `SendTargetResolver` port

**No direct dependency:** The task feature doesn't directly import from the connection feature. It relies on the send feature to handle connection resolution through the `SendTargetResolver` adapter port.

**TransportTargetSnapshot interface:**
```typescript
interface TransportTargetSnapshot {
  readonly targetId: string;
  readonly connectionId: string;
  readonly kind: TransportKind;
  readonly role: TransportTargetRole;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly routeLabel: string;
  readonly available: boolean;
}
```

### 4. **Frame Feature Consumption** (`/mnt/d/code/frontend/dongfanghong/rewrite/src/features/frame/`)

**Types consumed:**
- `ReadonlyFrameAsset` - Used in `FrameReader` port for reading frame definitions

**No direct dependency in task core:** The task feature defines a `FrameReader` adapter port but doesn't directly consume frame types in its core logic. The frame dependency is abstracted through the adapter pattern.

**FrameReader interface:**
```typescript
interface FrameReader {
  getFrameAsset(id: string): ReadonlyFrameAsset | undefined;
}
```

### 5. **Receive Event Flow (ConditionMatchInput)**

**Core type defined in task feature:**
```typescript
interface ConditionMatchInput {
  readonly frameId: string;
  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
  readonly sourceId?: string;
}
```

**Usage:**
- Used in `ReceiveEventSource.subscribe()` for condition matching
- Processed by `ConditionRegistry` in task-service.ts
- Evaluated against `WaitCondition` definitions using `evaluateCondition()` function
- Enables task steps to wait for specific frame field values before proceeding

### 6. **Implementation Usage Patterns**

**In task-service.ts:**
```typescript
interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {
  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider;
  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource;
  // state and now are optional utilities
}

// Send service is used via step executors
const stepExecutors = createStepExecutors({
  sendService,  // Used for send steps
  conditionRegistry,
  now,
});

// Receive source is subscribed to during task execution
receiveSubscription = receiveSource.subscribe((input) => {
  conditionRegistry.processInput(input);
});
```

**In task-step-executors.ts:**
```typescript
// Send steps use the send service directly
async function executeSendStep(...): Promise<TaskStepResult> {
  const request = buildSendRequest(step, definition, stepIndex);
  const sendResult = await ctx.sendService.execute(request);
  return { kind: 'send', stepIndex, iteration, sendResult };
}

// Wait-condition steps use the condition registry (fed by receive source)
async function executeWaitConditionStep(...): Promise<TaskStepResult> {
  const entry = ctx.conditionRegistry.register(condition, (value) => {
    // Called when ConditionMatchInput matches
    resolve({ result: { kind: 'wait-condition', matched: true, ... } });
  });
}
```

### Summary

The task feature maintains clean separation of concerns through the **adapter pattern**:

1. **Send dependency**: Minimal - only needs `SendRequest`/`SendResult` types and a simple `execute()` interface
2. **Connection dependency**: Abstracted away - handled entirely by the send feature
3. **Frame dependency**: Abstracted through `FrameReader` port - only needs to read frame definitions
4. **Receive dependency**: Decoupled through `ConditionMatchInput` - task defines the interface it needs, receive feature adapts to it

This design allows the task feature to remain independent of implementation details in the send, connection, and frame features, making it easier to test and maintain.
```

> AGENT

## 核实结论

你的直觉**部分正确**——task 功能的大量代码已经写好，但 task-real 设计文档中的**核心类型体系重构尚未实施**。具体来说：

### 已实现（旧类型体系上）
- task service 完整骨架（dispatch → 3 个独立循环）
- AND/OR 条件组注册 + `evaluateConditionGroup`
- Step executors（send/wait-condition/delay）
- Readiness 补丁（validation/builders/serialization/retry/stopAll/selectors）
- 70+ 测试

### 未实现（仅在设计文档中）
- `ScheduleDriver` discriminated union（代码仍用 `TaskSchedulingMode: 'timed'|'trigger'|'sequence'`）
- `StepRepeat` 类型
- `FieldVariation` 类型
- 统一 `runTask` 引擎（代码仍是 3 个独立循环分发）
- `StopGuard` / `executeRepeatableSend`
- `timerService` / `fieldValueProvider` 注入

### 审计判定
- **5 个 BLOCKER**：全部是类型体系重构相关
- 审计建议：UI 页面可以先基于旧类型推进，task-real 重构可并行独立进行

### UI 设计文档状态
- brainstorm 写在**新类型体系**上（引用 ScheduleDriver、FieldVariation 等）
- design doc 混用新旧术语

1	# task-real brainstorm
2	
3	> 日期：2026-05-09
4	> 状态：结论已锁定，ready for cs-feat-design
5	> Lane：Lane B（单 feature design，不拆 roadmap）
6	
7	## 结论
8	
9	task-real 是一个**统一执行引擎**，接收 TaskDefinition（step 序列 + 调度配置），按策略执行，产出 TaskInstanceCompletion 事件。
10	
11	核心设计选择：
12	
13	1. **ScheduleDriver × stopCondition × step.repeat 三层控制**，替代旧三种模式枚举
14	2. **send/wait-condition/delay 三种 step**，SCOE 指令用 step 序列组合，无专属 step type
15	3. **ConditionTerm + logicOperator** 支持单层 AND/OR，复用 shared/condition-operators
16	4. **fieldVariations 自动决定迭代次数**
17	5. **单帧发送走同引擎**，仅 UI 层简化
18	
19	## 事实与证据
20	
21	### 旧系统三种模式
22	
23	来源：`src/stores/frames/sendTasksStore.ts`、`useSendTaskExecutor.ts`、`useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts`
24	
25	| 模式 | 旧配置 | 旧行为 |
26	|------|--------|--------|
27	| 顺序发送 | `FrameInstanceInTask[]` | 单次，按序发完 |
28	| 定时循环 | `sendInterval + repeatCount + isInfinite` | 固定次数或无限 |
29	| 条件触发 | `triggerFrameId + conditions[] + responseDelay` | 监听接收帧，条件满足后发送 |
30	
31	旧系统条件组合：`TriggerCondition.logicOperator: 'and' | 'or'`（`sendInstances.ts:123`、`useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts:115-155`）。所有 SCOE 完成条件检查为 AND 遍历。
32	
33	### 旧 SCOE 参数关联机制
34	
35	来源：`scoeFrame.ts:366-491`、`sendFrame.ts:36-46`、`receiveCommand.ts:70-80`
36	
37	流程：接收指令帧 → `extractAndResolveParams()` 解析参数 → 参数值通过 `targetFieldId` 赋给发送帧字段 → 完成条件通过 `targetParamId` + 索引匹配关联参数选项。
38	
39	**关键结论**：参数值在 SCOE adapter 翻译时已知，可直接 resolve 为 literal 值填入 TaskDefinition。task 引擎不需要理解参数关联。
40	
41	### 旧文件发送模式
42	
43	来源：`readFileAndSend.ts:45-135`
44	
45	流程：参数值含 `.txt` → 读文件按行分割 → 逗号分隔值 split → 构造 `FieldVariation[]` → 创建定时任务 `repeatCount = values.length`。
46	
47	### 已有 task 骨架状态
48	
49	`rewrite/src/features/task/` 下已有完整实现骨架：类型系统、服务合约（7 方法）、三层调度循环、多态 step 执行、错误策略引擎、条件匹配 + O(1) 索引、adapter ports、state + 6 个 selector。
50	
51	**主要变更**：类型重组（TaskSchedulingMode → ScheduleDriver + stopCondition），ConditionRegistry 升级（单条件 → AND/OR），新增 step.repeat 能力。估计涉及 6-8 个文件、300+ 行已有代码。
52	
53	## 设计决策
54	
55	### 决策 1：去掉 IterationControl，用 stopCondition + step.repeat 替代
56	
57	**理由**：IterationControl.fixed(N) 与 stopCondition.maxIterations 语义完全重复。去掉 IterationControl，迭代行为由三个正交机制表达：
58	
59	- ScheduleDriver：每轮 step 序列如何触发（immediate / timer / event）
60	- stopCondition：整个任务何时停止（maxIterations / maxDurationMs / exitCondition）
61	- step.repeat：单个 send step 的重复发送（until / maxCount）
62	
63	**旧模式映射**：
64	
65	| 旧模式 | 新表达 |
66	|--------|--------|
67	| 顺序发送 | `schedule: immediate`，无 stopCondition，无 repeat |
68	| 定时固定 N 次 | `schedule: timer(intervalMs)` + `stopCondition: { maxIterations: N }` |
69	| 定时无限 | `schedule: timer(intervalMs)`，无 stopCondition |
70	| 条件触发 | `schedule: event(conditions)` |
71	| 一直发 A 直到 X，再发 B | `schedule: immediate`，step A `repeat.until: X`，step B 无 repeat |
72	| 定时发送整个序列直到 Y | `schedule: timer` + `stopCondition: { exitCondition: Y }` |
73	| 文件逐行发送 | `fieldVariations` 自动 maxIterations + `schedule: timer` |
74	| 单帧发送 | `schedule: immediate`，一个 send step，无 repeat |
75	
76	### 决策 2：step.repeat 只加在 send step 上
77	
78	**理由**：wait-condition 本身就是"重复检查直到条件满足"的语义，delay 重复无意义。repeat 只加在 send step 上，不引入独立的 repeat wrapper type。
79	
80	### 决策 3：OR 组合保留
81	
82	**理由**：旧系统 OR 组合有完整的类型定义、运行时逻辑和 UI 入口：
83	- 类型：`TriggerCondition.logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or'`（`sendInstances.ts:123`）
84	- 运行时：`useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts:149` 使用 `condition.logicOperator` 做短路 AND/OR 评估
85	- UI：`TriggerConditionList.vue:36` 有 AND/OR 下拉选项；`TriggerSendDialog.vue:503` 显示逻辑操作符
86	- 默认值始终为 `'and'`
87	
88	通过 ConditionTerm.logicOperator 可选字段表达，默认 AND。
89	
90	### 决策 4：correlate 不做
91	
92	**理由**：旧 SCOE 参数关联是 SCOE adapter 内部逻辑。adapter 在翻译时 resolve 参数值为 literal，构造 TaskDefinition 时所有阈值已知。task 引擎不需要 step 间值传递。
93	
94	### 决策 5：FieldValueSpec.expression 不做
95	
96	**理由**：表达式求值归 send feature（SendRequest.variables）。task 的 fieldVariations 是静态值数组，按 roundIndex 取值。动态计算用表达式时由 send 侧处理。
97	
98	### 决策 6：fieldVariations 自动决定迭代
99	
100	**理由**：fieldVariations 存在时，stopCondition.maxIterations 自动设为 `max(values.length)`。消费方不需要手动对齐。
101	
102	### 决策 7：单帧发送走同引擎
103	
104	**理由**：单帧发送 = `schedule: immediate` + 一个 send step + 无 stopCondition。UI 给简化表单，背后构造最简 TaskDefinition。好处：验证、错误处理、SendResult 全走同一路径，不维护两套发送逻辑。
105	
106	### 决策 8：SCOE 保持"翻译成 TaskDefinition"单一职责
107	
108	**理由**：SCOE feature 唯一职责是把外部指令翻译成 TaskDefinition。参数解析、文件读取、命令映射全部在 SCOE 内部完成。翻译后的 TaskDefinition 与 UI 创建的无区别。可复用 ConditionTerm 等类型，但模块边界不交叉。
109	
110	**非 task 场景说明**：部分 SCOE 命令（health_check、link_check 等）不需要创建 task，指令接入 feature 自行处理这些轻量查询/状态类命令。task-real 只负责"需要执行 step 序列"的命令，指令接入 feature 决定哪些命令走 task 路径、哪些自行处理。这不在 task-real 范围内。
111	
112	### 决策 9：TimerService 放 platform facade
113	
114	**理由**：renderer 进程 setInterval 受主线程阻塞和后台节流影响。Web Worker 实现精度 ~1ms，不跨 IPC 边界，不违反"main process 不承载领域规则"。task core 通过接口依赖，单元测试用 fake。具体位置待确认（platform/ 下独立模块或挂已有子目录）。
115	
116	## 最终类型模型
117	
118	```typescript
119	interface TaskDefinition {
120	  id: string;
121	  name: string;
122	  steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
123	  schedule: ScheduleDriver;
124	  stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
125	  fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];
126	  errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
127	}
128	
129	type ScheduleDriver =
130	  | { kind: 'immediate' }
131	  | { kind: 'timer'; intervalMs: number }
132	  | { kind: 'event'; conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[] };
133	
134	interface TaskStopCondition {
135	  maxIterations?: number;
136	  maxDurationMs?: number;
137	  exitCondition?: readonly ConditionTerm[];
138	}
139	
140	type TaskStepDefinition =
141	  | { kind: 'send'; id: string; config: SendStepConfig }
142	  | { kind: 'wait-condition'; id: string; config: WaitConditionConfig }
143	  | { kind: 'delay'; id: string; config: { durationMs: number } };
144	
145	interface SendStepConfig {
146	  frameId: string;
147	  targetId: string;
148	  userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;  // 对应 SendRequest.userFieldValues
149	  variables?: VariableMap;  // 对应 SendRequest.variables，传给 send 表达式引擎
150	  context?: SendContext;    // 对应 SendRequest.context，传递 taskId/stepIndex 上下文
151	  intervalAfterMs?: number;
152	  repeat?: {
153	    intervalMs: number;
154	    until?: readonly ConditionTerm[];
155	    maxCount?: number;
156	  };
157	}
158	
159	interface WaitConditionConfig {
160	  conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];
161	  timeoutMs?: number;
162	  onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';
163	}
164	
165	interface ConditionTerm {
166	  // 现有 WaitCondition 重命名 + 扩展 logicOperator
167	  // 旧→新映射：WaitCondition → ConditionTerm（字段一一对应）
168	  frameId: string;
169	  fieldId: string;
170	  operator: ComparisonOperator;
171	  threshold: string | number;
172	  sourceId?: string;
173	  logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or';  // 新增，旧系统有 AND/OR 证据
174	}
175	
176	interface FieldVariation {
177	  fieldId: string;
178	  values: readonly (string | number)[];
179	}
180	
181	interface TaskErrorPolicy {
182	  onFailure: 'retry' | 'skip-step' | 'stop' | 'pause';
183	  retryCount?: number;
184	  retryDelayMs?: number;
185	}
186	```
187	
188	辅助函数：
189	
190	```typescript
191	function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): TaskStopCondition {
192	  if (def.fieldVariations?.length) {
193	    const maxLen = Math.max(...def.fieldVariations.map(v => v.values.length));
194	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: maxLen };
195	  }
196	  return def.stopCondition ?? {};
197	}
198	```
199	
200	循环引擎核心逻辑：
201	
202	```typescript
203	async function runTask(ctx: TaskContext) {
204	  const driver = createDriver(ctx.definition.schedule);
205	  const stop = createStopGuard(resolveStopCondition(ctx.definition));
206	
207	  while (!stop.shouldStop(ctx.state)) {
208	    await driver.waitForNext(ctx);
209	
210	    for (const step of ctx.definition.steps) {
211	      if (stop.shouldStop(ctx.state)) break;
212	
213	      if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.repeat) {
214	        await executeRepeatableSend(ctx, step, stop);
215	      } else {
216	        await executeStep(ctx, step);
217	      }
218	    }
219	    ctx.state = advanceIteration(ctx.state);
220	    injectFieldVariations(ctx);
221	  }
222	}
223	```
224	
225	## 边界
226	
227	### task 拥有
228	
229	- 通用执行引擎：step 序列执行、错误处理、进度追踪
230	- ScheduleDriver + stopCondition + step.repeat 控制
231	- 条件匹配逻辑（复用 shared/condition-operators）
232	- task/case/step lifecycle 和执行进度
233	- fieldVariations 按轮次注入
234	
235	### task 不拥有
236	
237	- 页面工作台状态（UI 归 pages）
238	- 发送链局部细节（send owns）
239	- receive 解析（receive owns）
240	- 对外回执协议（northbound owns）
241	- SCOE 领域规则和参数解析（SCOE owns）
242	- 报告生成（report owns）
243	- 帧定义管理（frame owns，task 只引用 frameId）
244	- TimerService 实现（platform owns）
245	
246	### Feature 间交互
247	
248	| 方向 | 交互方式 | 说明 |
249	|------|---------|------|
250	| task → send | `SendServiceProvider.execute(req): Promise<SendResult>` | task 构造 SendRequest（含 `context: { source: 'task', taskId, stepIndex }`），await 结果 |
251	| task → receive | `ReceiveEventSource` adapter port | event driver 和 wait-condition 订阅接收事件 |
252	| task → frame | `FrameAssetReader` 引用 | 构造 TaskDefinition 时查询帧列表和字段 |
253	| task → result | emit `TaskInstanceCompletion` | result 只消费此事件，不直接消费 raw events |
254	| send → task | 无反向依赖 | — |
255	| receive → task | 无反向依赖 | — |
256	| result → task | 无反向依赖，只读 selector | — |
257	
258	## 不做什么
259	
260	- 不做 correlate / step 间值传递
261	- 不做表达式动态字段值（fieldVariations 够用）
262	- 不做嵌套任务 / 动态步骤
263	- 不做 SCOE 专属 step type 或专属逻辑
264	- 不做 UI / runtime 装配 / storage
265	- 不做任务模板 / 执行历史持久化
266	- 不做多任务并发协调（依赖 send queue）
267	
268	## 已有类型迁移映射
269	
270	来源：`rewrite/src/features/task/core/types.ts`
271	
272	| 旧类型/字段 | 新类型/字段 | 说明 |
273	|-------------|------------|------|
274	| `TaskSchedulingMode: 'timed' \| 'trigger' \| 'sequence'` | `ScheduleDriver` discriminated union | 旧 `'sequence'` → `{ kind: 'immediate' }`；旧 `'timed'` → `{ kind: 'timer', intervalMs }`；旧 `'trigger'` → `{ kind: 'event', conditions }` |
275	| `TaskDefinition.schedulingMode` | `TaskDefinition.schedule` | 字段重命名 |
276	| `TaskDefinition.intervalMs` | `ScheduleDriver.timer.intervalMs` | 从平铺字段移入 timer 变体 |
277	| `TaskDefinition.triggerCondition?: WaitCondition` | `ScheduleDriver.event.conditions: ConditionTerm[]` | 单条件 → 条件数组 |
278	| `TaskDefinition.triggerSource: TaskTriggerSource` | 移除 | 信息性标识，不影响执行逻辑 |
279	| `WaitCondition` | `ConditionTerm` | 重命名 + 新增 `logicOperator` |
280	| `WaitConditionStepConfig.condition: WaitCondition` | `WaitConditionConfig.conditions: ConditionTerm[]` | 单条件 → 条件数组 |
281	| `SendStepConfig.fieldValues` | `SendStepConfig.userFieldValues` | 对齐 send-real 的 `SendRequest.userFieldValues` 命名 |
282	| （无） | `SendStepConfig.variables?: VariableMap` | 新增，传给 send 表达式引擎 |
283	| `TaskStopCondition.maxIterations` | 保留，同时替代旧的 IterationControl.fixed | |
284	| （无） | `TaskStopCondition.exitCondition` | 新增条件退出 |
285	| （无） | `SendStepConfig.repeat` | 新增单步重复 |
286	| （无） | `SendStepConfig.intervalAfterMs` | 新增步骤间间隔 |
287	| `TaskDefinition` 平铺调度字段 | `FieldVariation[]` | 新增 fieldVariations |
288	
289	涉及文件（task feature 下共 26 个文件）：
290	
291	- **core/** `types.ts`（major，类型重组）、`condition-matcher.ts`（major，AND/OR）、`progress.ts`（minor）、`lifecycle.ts`（minor）、`clone.ts`（minor）、`index.ts`（跟随导出）
292	- **services/** `task-iteration-loops.ts`（major，三种循环→统一循环）、`condition-registry.ts`（major，AND/OR 组合）、`task-step-executors.ts`（major，repeat + context + userFieldValues）、`task-service.ts`（minor，内部适配）、`task-error-policy.ts`（no-change）、`ta[REDACTED_SK].ts`（minor）、`index.ts`（跟随导出）
293	- **adapters/** `ports.ts`（minor，新增 TimerService）、`fake-send-service.ts`（minor，适配 context）、`fake-receive-event-source.ts`（no-change）、`test-exports.ts`（跟随）、`index.ts`（跟随）
294	- **state/** `task-state.ts`（minor，类型跟随）、`index.ts`（跟随）
295	- **selectors/** `task-selectors.ts`（minor，类型跟随）、`index.ts`（跟随）
296	- **fixtures/** `task-fixtures.ts`（minor，适配新类型）
297	- **__tests__/** `task-core.spec.ts`（minor，类型跟随）、`ta[REDACTED_SK].spec.ts`（minor，类型跟随）
298	- **index.ts**（跟随导出）
299	
300	## 给 design 的输入清单
301	
302	1. 最终类型模型（上文已列出）
303	2. 循环引擎统一实现方案（driver 策略 + stopGuard + step repeat）
304	3. ConditionRegistry 升级（单条件 → AND/OR 组合匹配）
305	4. fieldVariations 注入时机（每轮迭代开始前）
306	5. TimerService adapter 接口设计（platform facade，Web Worker 实现）
307	6. 已有代码迁移映射表（旧类型 → 新类型）
308	7. 与 send-real / receive-real / frame-real / result 的 public API 对接确认
309	8. 翻译约定：startDelay / responseDelay → delay step 或 intervalAfterMs
310	9. SCOE adapter 翻译规则（参数 resolve + 文件读取 → FieldVariation + 命令映射）
311	10. 单帧发送的 TaskDefinition 构造约定
312	
313	## 需求覆盖度
314	
315	### 旧系统可观测行为（20 项）
316	
317	17 项完全满足，3 项通过翻译约定满足：
318	
319	| # | 需求 | 满足方式 |
320	|---|------|---------|
321	| A6 | 启动延时 | 首个 delay step 或 intervalAfterMs |
322	| A11 | 触发后响应延时 | steps 首个 delay step |
323	| A19 | SCOE 参数化完成条件 | SCOE adapter 翻译时 resolve 为 literal |
324	
325	### 架构约束（12 项）
326	
327	全部满足。core 层零 Vue/Pinia/Electron 依赖，selector 不可变，stop → stopped。
328	
329	### 待外部确认
330	
331	- TimerService 在 platform/ 下的具体位置
332	
```

```
1	# task-real design
2	
3	> 日期：2026-05-09
4	> 状态：draft
5	> Lane：Lane B（单 feature design）
6	> 直接合同：`codestable/features/rewrite-task/task-real-brainstorm.md`
7	> Checklist：`codestable/features/rewrite-task/rewrite-task-checklist.yaml`
8	> 边界护栏：CLAUDE.md、`codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`、`codestable/quality/rewrite-quality-rules.md`
9	
10	## 设计摘要
11	
12	task-real 将现有三种独立调度循环（timed/trigger/sequence）统一为 `ScheduleDriver` discriminated union + `stopCondition` + `step.repeat` 三层控制。主要变更：类型重组、ConditionRegistry 升级为 AND/OR 组合匹配、send step 增加 repeat 能力、`buildSendRequest` 对齐实际 `SendRequest` API。
13	
14	---
15	
16	## 1. 类型系统
17	
18	### 1.1 新增类型
19	
20	#### ScheduleDriver
21	
22	替代 `TaskSchedulingMode` + `TaskDefinition` 上的平铺调度字段（`intervalMs`、`triggerCondition`、`delayBeforeStartMs`、`cooldownMs`）。
23	
24	```typescript
25	type ScheduleDriver =
26	  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
27	  | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
28	  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number };
29	```
30	
31	旧→新映射：
32	
33	| 旧模式 | 新表达 |
34	|--------|--------|
35	| `'sequence'` + 无额外字段 | `{ kind: 'immediate' }` |
36	| `'timed'` + `intervalMs` | `{ kind: 'timer', intervalMs }` |
37	| `'trigger'` + `triggerCondition` + `cooldownMs` | `{ kind: 'event', conditions: [...], cooldownMs }` |
38	
39	来源：brainstorm 决策 1
40	
41	#### ConditionTerm
42	
43	扩展 `WaitCondition`，新增 `logicOperator`。
44	
45	```typescript
46	interface ConditionTerm {
47	  readonly frameId: string;
48	  readonly fieldId: string;
49	  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
50	  readonly threshold: string | number;
51	  readonly sourceId?: string;
52	  readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or';  // 新增，默认 'and'
53	}
54	```
55	
56	字段一一对应 `WaitCondition`（core/types.ts:26-32），新增 `logicOperator`。
57	
58	`logicOperator` 语义：`conditions[i].logicOperator` 决定当前条件如何与 `conditions[0..i-1]` 的累积结果组合。`conditions[0].logicOperator` 无意义（被忽略）。默认 `'and'`。
59	
60	来源：brainstorm 决策 3，旧系统 `TriggerCondition.logicOperator` 证据（`sendInstances.ts:123`、`useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts:115-155`）
61	
62	#### FieldVariation
63	
64	```typescript
65	interface FieldVariation {
66	  readonly fieldId: string;
67	  readonly values: readonly (string | number)[];
68	}
69	```
70	
71	按迭代轮次覆盖 `SendStepConfig.userFieldValues` 中对应字段。`stopCondition.maxIterations` 自动设为 `max(fieldVariations[].values.length)`。
72	
73	来源：brainstorm 决策 6，旧系统 `readFileAndSend.ts` FieldVariation 翻译
74	
75	#### StepRepeat
76	
77	```typescript
78	interface StepRepeat {
79	  readonly intervalMs: number;
80	  readonly until?: readonly ConditionTerm[];
81	  readonly maxCount?: number;
82	}
83	```
84	
85	只用于 send step。来源：brainstorm 决策 2。
86	
87	### 1.2 变更类型
88	
89	#### TaskDefinition — major
90	
91	```typescript
92	interface TaskDefinition {
93	  readonly id: string;
94	  readonly name: string;
95	  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
96	  readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver;                          // 替代 schedulingMode + 平铺字段
97	  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
98	  readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];       // 新增
99	  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
100	}
101	```
102	
103	移除字段（对比 core/types.ts:113-126）：
104	
105	| 移除字段 | 原类型/位置 | 替代 |
106	|----------|------------|------|
107	| `schedulingMode` | `TaskSchedulingMode` | `schedule: ScheduleDriver` |
108	| `triggerSource` | `TaskTriggerSource` | 移除（信息性标识） |
109	| `targetId?` | `string` | 移到 `SendStepConfig.targetId` |
110	| `intervalMs?` | `number` | `ScheduleDriver.timer.intervalMs` |
111	| `delayBeforeStartMs?` | `number` | 首个 delay step |
112	| `triggerCondition?` | `WaitCondition` | `ScheduleDriver.event.conditions` |
113	| `cooldownMs?` | `number` | `ScheduleDriver.event.cooldownMs` |
114	
115	新增字段：`schedule`、`fieldVariations`。
116	
117	#### SendStepConfig — major
118	
119	```typescript
120	interface SendStepConfig {
121	  readonly frameId: string;
122	  readonly targetId: string;                                                    // 必需（原可选 + fallback definition.targetId）
123	  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;  // 重命名 fieldValues → userFieldValues
124	  readonly variables?: VariableMap;                                             // 新增
125	  readonly intervalAfterMs?: number;                                            // 新增
126	  readonly repeat?: StepRepeat;                                                 // 新增
127	}
128	```
129	
130	对齐 `SendRequest`（send/core/types.ts:SendRequest）：
131	
132	| SendStepConfig 字段 | SendRequest 字段 | 说明 |
133	|--------------------|-----------------|------|
134	| `frameId` | `frameId: string` | 直接映射 |
135	| `targetId` | `targetId: string` | 直接映射（现必需） |
136	| `userFieldValues` | `userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>` | 对齐命名，`SendFieldValue = string \| number \| boolean` |
137	| `variables` | `variables?: VariableMap` | 直接映射，`VariableMap = ReadonlyMap<string, VariableValue>`（shared/expression/types.ts） |
138	| （构造） | `context?: SendContext` | 由 `buildSendRequest` 构造，不暴露给配置 |
139	| 移除 `options` | — | `SendRequest` 无此字段 |
140	| `intervalAfterMs` | — | task 内部消费 |
141	| `repeat` | — | task 内部消费 |
142	
143	#### WaitConditionStepConfig → WaitConditionConfig — major
144	
145	```typescript
146	interface WaitConditionConfig {
147	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];   // 单条件 → 条件数组
148	  readonly timeoutMs?: number;                     // 可选（undefined = 无限等待直到信号中断）
149	  readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';
150	}
151	```
152	
153	变更（对比 core/types.ts:43-47）：
154	- `condition: WaitCondition` → `conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]`
155	- `timeoutMs: number` → `timeoutMs?: number`
156	
157	#### TaskStepDefinition — minor
158	
159	```typescript
160	type TaskStepDefinition =
161	  | { readonly kind: 'send';    readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: SendStepConfig }
162	  | { readonly kind: 'wait-condition'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: WaitConditionConfig }
163	  | { readonly kind: 'delay';   readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: DelayStepConfig };
164	```
165	
166	变更（对比 core/types.ts:53-77）：
167	- `sendConfig` → `config`，`waitConfig` → `config`，`delayConfig` → `config`
168	- `name?` 保留（display concern）
169	
170	#### TaskStopCondition — minor
171	
172	```typescript
173	interface TaskStopCondition {
174	  readonly maxIterations?: number;
175	  readonly maxDurationMs?: number;
176	  readonly exitCondition?: readonly ConditionTerm[];  // 新增
177	}
178	```
179	
180	对比 core/types.ts:106-109：新增 `exitCondition`。
181	
182	#### ConditionMatchInput — breaking
183	
184	```typescript
185	interface ConditionMatchInput {
186	  readonly frameId: string;
187	  readonly sourceId?: string;
188	  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;  // 替代 fieldId + value
189	}
190	```
191	
192	对比 core/types.ts:223-228：移除 `fieldId`、`value`，新增 `fieldValues`。
193	
194	理由：AND/OR 组合条件需要一次评估多个字段。单字段 input 不满足需求。与旧系统行为一致（旧系统在每帧事件中评估所有条件，`useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts:115-155`）。
195	
196	### 1.3 移除类型
197	
198	| 移除项 | 原位置（core/types.ts） | 替代 |
199	|--------|----------------------|------|
200	| `TaskSchedulingMode` | :91 | `ScheduleDriver` |
201	| `TASK_SCHEDULING_MODES` | :90 | 无需常量 |
202	| `TaskTriggerSource` | :88 | 无替代 |
203	| `TASK_TRIGGER_SOURCES` | :81-87 | 无需常量 |
204	| `WaitCondition` | :26-32 | `ConditionTerm` |
205	| `WaitConditionStepConfig` | :43-47 | `WaitConditionConfig` |
206	| `SendStepConfig.options` | :40 | 移除 |
207	
208	### 1.4 不变类型
209	
210	| 类型 | 说明 |
211	|------|------|
212	| `TaskStepKind` | `'send' \| 'wait-condition' \| 'delay'` |
213	| `ComparisonOperator` | 完全不变，与 shared/condition-operators/types.ts 一致 |
214	| `TaskErrorPolicy` / `TaskErrorAction` | 完全不变 |
215	| `TaskLifecycleStatus` / `LifecycleAction` | 完全不变 |
216	| `SendStepResult` | 不变 |
217	| `WaitConditionStepResult` | 不变 |
218	| `DelayStepResult` | 不变 |
219	| `DelayStepConfig` | 不变 |
220	| `TaskStepResult` | 不变 |
221	
222	### 1.5 辅助函数
223	
224	```typescript
225	type ResolvedStopCondition = TaskStopCondition;
226	
227	function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition {
228	  if (def.fieldVariations && def.fieldVariations.length > 0) {
229	    const maxLen = Math.max(...def.fieldVariations.map(v => v.values.length));
230	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: maxLen };
231	  }
232	  return def.stopCondition ?? {};
233	}
234	```
235	
236	`resolveStopCondition` 放在 `core/types.ts` 中（与 TaskDefinition/TaskStopCondition 同文件），通过 `core/index.ts` 导出。
237	
238	---
239	
240	## 2. 循环引擎
241	
242	### 2.1 统一 runTask
243	
244	替代 `runTimedLoop`、`runTriggerLoop`、`runSequenceLoop`（task-iteration-loops.ts:106-233）。
245	
246	```typescript
247	interface TaskExecutionContext {
248	  readonly definition: TaskDefinition;
249	  readonly resolvedStop: ResolvedStopCondition;
250	  state: TaskStateContainer;
251	  stepExecutors: StepExecutors;
252	  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;
253	  updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn;
254	  errorPolicy: ErrorPolicyHandler;
255	  fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
256	  timerService?: TimerService;
257	  now: () => string;
258	}
259	
260	async function runTask(
261	  instanceId: string,
262	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
263	  signal: Promise<void>,
264	): Promise<void> {
265	  const driver = createDriver(ctx.definition.schedule, ctx, instanceId, signal);
266	  const stopGuard = createStopGuard(ctx.resolvedStop, ctx);
267	
268	  let iteration = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId)?.currentIteration ?? 0;
269	
270	  while (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {
271	    const triggered = await driver.waitForNext(instanceId, iteration, signal);
272	    if (!triggered) break;
273	
274	    const completed = await executeSteps(instanceId, iteration, ctx, signal);
275	    if (!completed) break;
276	
277	    if (stopGuard.checkExitCondition()) break;
278	
279	    iteration++;
280	    ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentIteration: iteration });
281	  }
282	
283	  driver.dispose();
284	}
285	```
286	
287	### 2.2 ScheduleDriver 实现
288	
289	```typescript
290	interface ScheduleDriverAdapter {
291	  waitForNext(instanceId: string, iteration: number, signal: Promise<void>): Promise<boolean>;
292	  dispose(): void;
293	}
294	```
295	
296	#### immediate
297	
298	```typescript
299	{ waitForNext: async () => true, dispose: () => {} }
300	```
301	
302	#### timer
303	
304	```typescript
305	function createTimerDriver(intervalMs: number): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
306	  let firstCall = true;
307	
308	  return {
309	    async waitForNext(_id, _iter, signal) {
310	      if (firstCall) { firstCall = false; return true; }
311	
312	      const delay = sleep(intervalMs);
313	      const race = await Promise.race([
314	        delay.promise.then(() => true),
315	        signal.then(() => false),
316	      ]);
317	      if (!race) delay.cancel();
318	      return race;
319	    },
320	    dispose() {},
321	  };
322	}
323	```
324	
325	对比现有 `runTimedLoop`（task-iteration-loops.ts:106-158）：
326	- 首次调用无延迟（替代原 `delayBeforeStartMs`，现由 delay step 表达）
327	- 后续调用等待 intervalMs
328	- 通过 signal 支持中断
329	
330	#### event
331	
332	```typescript
333	function createEventDriver(
334	  schedule: Extract<ScheduleDriver, { kind: 'event' }>,
335	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
336	  instanceId: string,
337	  signal: Promise<void>,
338	): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
339	  const { conditions, cooldownMs = 0 } = schedule;
340	  let lastTriggerTime = 0;
341	  let notifyResolve: ((value: boolean) => void) | null = null;
342	  let disposed = false;
343	
344	  const group = ctx.conditionRegistry.registerGroup(conditions, () => {
345	    if (disposed) return;
346	    const now = Date.now();
347	    if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && now - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;
348	    lastTriggerTime = now;
349	    if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(true); notifyResolve = null; }
350	  });
351	
352	  return {
353	    async waitForNext() {
354	      if (disposed) return false;
355	      return Promise.race([
356	        new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => { notifyResolve = resolve; }),
357	        signal.then(() => false),
358	      ]);
359	    },
360	    dispose() {
361	      disposed = true;
362	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
363	      if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(false); notifyResolve = null; }
364	    },
365	  };
366	}
367	```
368	
369	对比现有 `runTriggerLoop`（task-iteration-loops.ts:160-223）：
370	- 从 `register(condition, handler)` → `registerGroup(conditions, handler)`
371	- cooldownMs 逻辑从循环体内移到 driver 内部
372	- triggerQueue 机制简化为 Promise resolve
373	
374	### 2.3 StopGuard
375	
376	```typescript
377	interface StopGuard {
378	  shouldStop(instanceId: string, iteration: number): boolean;
379	  checkExitCondition(): boolean;
380	}
381	
382	function createStopGuard(stop: ResolvedStopCondition, ctx: TaskExecutionContext): StopGuard {
383	  const startTime = Date.now();
384	
385	  return {
386	    shouldStop(instanceId, iteration) {
387	      const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
388	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return true;
389	      if (stop.maxIterations !== undefined && iteration >= stop.maxIterations) return true;
390	      if (stop.maxDurationMs !== undefined && Date.now() - startTime >= stop.maxDurationMs) return true;
391	      return false;
392	    },
393	
394	    checkExitCondition() {
395	      if (!stop.exitCondition) return false;
396	      if (!ctx.fieldValueProvider) return false;
397	      return evaluateConditionGroup(stop.exitCondition, ctx.fieldValueProvider());
398	    },
399	  };
400	}
401	```
402	
403	`checkExitCondition` 需要 `fieldValueProvider`（由 runtime 层从 `ReceiveReader.listFieldValues()` 提供，测试用 fake）。
404	
405	### 2.4 step repeat
406	
407	```typescript
408	async function executeRepeatableSend(
409	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
410	  instanceId: string,
411	  step: SendStepDefinition,
412	  iteration: number,
413	  stepIndex: number,
414	  signal: Promise<void>,
415	): Promise<boolean> {
416	  const { repeat } = step.config;
417	  if (!repeat) {
418	    const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex);
419	    ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);
420	    return result.sendResult.kind === 'sent';
421	  }
422	
423	  let count = 0;
424	  const maxCount = repeat.maxCount ?? Infinity;
425	
426	  while (count < maxCount) {
427	    const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex);
428	    ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);
429	
430	    if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') return false;
431	
432	    count++;
433	
434	    if (repeat.until && ctx.fieldValueProvider) {
435	      if (evaluateConditionGroup(repeat.until, ctx.fieldValueProvider())) break;
436	    }
437	
438	    if (count < maxCount) {
439	      const delay = sleep(repeat.intervalMs);
440	      const race = await Promise.race([delay.promise.then(() => true), signal.then(() => false)]);
441	      if (!race) { delay.cancel(); return false; }
442	    }
443	  }
444	
445	  return true;
446	}
447	```
448	
449	### 2.5 fieldVariations 注入
450	
451	在 `buildSendRequest` 时合并，不是单独的注入步骤。
452	
453	```typescript
454	function resolveFieldValues(
455	  baseValues: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> | undefined,
456	  fieldVariations: readonly FieldVariation[] | undefined,
457	  iteration: number,
458	): Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> {
459	  if (!fieldVariations || fieldVariations.length === 0) return baseValues ?? {};
460	
461	  const result: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = { ...(baseValues ?? {}) };
462	  for (const variation of fieldVariations) {
463	    if (iteration < variation.values.length) {
464	      result[variation.fieldId] = variation.values[iteration];
465	    }
466	  }
467	  return result;
468	}
469	```
470	
471	---
472	
473	## 3. ConditionRegistry 升级
474	
475	### 3.1 evaluateConditionGroup
476	
477	替代现有 `evaluateCondition`（core/condition-matcher.ts），支持 AND/OR 组合。
478	
479	```typescript
480	function evaluateConditionGroup(
481	  conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[],
482	  fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>,
483	): boolean {
484	  if (conditions.length === 0) return true;
485	
486	  let result = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[0], fieldValues);
487	
488	  for (let i = 1; i < conditions.length; i++) {
489	    const op = conditions[i].logicOperator ?? 'and';
490	    const current = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[i], fieldValues);
491	
492	    if (op === 'or') {
493	      result = result || current;
494	      if (result) break;   // OR 短路
495	    } else {
496	      result = result && current;
497	      if (!result) break;  // AND 短路
498	    }
499	  }
500	
501	  return result;
502	}
503	
504	function evaluateSingleCondition(
505	  condition: ConditionTerm,
506	  fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>,
507	): boolean {
508	  const value = fieldValues[condition.fieldId];
509	  if (value === undefined || value === null) return false;
510	  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);
511	}
512	```
513	
514	来源：旧系统 `useSendTaskTriggerListener.ts:115-154` AND/OR 短路逻辑
515	依赖：`compareValues`（shared/condition-operators/index.ts:3）
516	
517	### 3.2 ConditionRegistry 改造
518	
519	从单条件注册（condition-registry.ts:11-56）改为条件组注册。
520	
521	```typescript
522	interface ConditionGroup {
523	  readonly id: string;
524	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];
525	  readonly onSatisfied: () => void;
526	}
527	
528	class ConditionRegistry {
529	  private groups = new Map<string, ConditionGroup>();
530	  private frameIndex = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
531	  private nextGroupId = 1;
532	
533	  registerGroup(conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[], onSatisfied: () => void): ConditionGroup {
534	    const id = `cg-${this.nextGroupId++}`;
535	    const group: ConditionGroup = { id, conditions, onSatisfied };
536	    this.groups.set(id, group);
537	
538	    const frameIds = new Set(conditions.map(c => c.frameId));
539	    for (const frameId of frameIds) {
540	      if (!this.frameIndex.has(frameId)) this.frameIndex.set(frameId, new Set());
541	      this.frameIndex.get(frameId)!.add(id);
542	    }
543	
544	    return group;
545	  }
546	
547	  unregisterGroup(group: ConditionGroup): void {
548	    this.groups.delete(group.id);
549	    for (const set of this.frameIndex.values()) set.delete(group.id);
550	  }
551	
552	  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
553	    const groupIds = this.frameIndex.get(input.frameId);
554	    if (!groupIds) return;
555	
556	    for (const groupId of groupIds) {
557	      const group = this.groups.get(groupId);
558	      if (!group) continue;
559	
560	      // sourceId 过滤
561	      if (input.sourceId !== undefined) {
562	        const hasSourceMatch = group.conditions.some(
563	          c => c.sourceId === undefined || c.sourceId === input.sourceId
564	        );
565	        if (!hasSourceMatch) continue;
566	      }
567	
568	      if (evaluateConditionGroup(group.conditions, input.fieldValues)) {
569	        group.onSatisfied();
570	      }
571	    }
572	  }
573	
574	  clear(): void {
575	    this.groups.clear();
576	    this.frameIndex.clear();
577	  }
578	
579	  get size(): number {
580	    return this.groups.size;
581	  }
582	}
583	```
584	
585	### 3.3 ConditionMatchInput 变更影响
586	
587	| 消费方 | 文件 | 影响 |
588	|--------|------|------|
589	| `ConditionRegistry.processInput` | services/condition-registry.ts | 签名不变，内部改为 group 评估 |
590	| `evaluateCondition` | core/condition-matcher.ts | 签名变更 |
591	| `fake-receive-event-source.ts` | adapters/ | 适配新 `ConditionMatchInput` |
592	| 测试 fixtures | fixtures/ | 适配新结构 |
593	
594	### 3.4 跨 feature 协调说明
595	
596	`ConditionMatchInput` 变更是 **task 内部变更**，不影响其他 feature 的公开 API。
597	
598	**数据流**：
599	```
600	receive-real (公开 API 不变)
601	  → runtime 层 ReceiveEventSource 适配器 (需更新)
602	    → task ConditionRegistry.processInput (内部变更)
603	```
604	
605	- `ConditionMatchInput` 定义在 `task/core/types.ts`，是 task feature 的内部类型
606	- `ReceiveEventSource` 接口定义在 `task/adapters/ports.ts`，也是 task 内部
607	- **receive-real 不需要任何变更**。receive 的 `ReceiveReader.listFieldValues()` / `ReceiveService.listEvents()` 公开 API 完全不变
608	- 需要变更的是 **runtime 层**的 `ReceiveEventSource` 适配器实现：从逐字段 emit 改为逐帧 emit（将 `ReceiveFieldValueSnapshot[]` 按 frameId 聚合为 `ConditionMatchInput.fieldValues`）
609	- **send-real 不需要任何变更**。`SendRequest` / `SendService.execute()` 公开 API 不变，是 task 的 `buildSendRequest` 对齐到已有的 `SendRequest` 签名
610	
611	**实施节奏**：task-real 可以独立完成所有变更（类型、引擎、测试），runtime 适配器在 runtime 装配阶段更新。不阻塞 task-real 实施。
612	
613	---
614	
615	## 4. Step 执行器
616	
617	### 4.1 buildSendRequest 对齐
618	
619	当前代码（task-step-executors.ts:17-29）与 `SendRequest`（send/core/types.ts）不对齐：
620	
621	| 当前代码 | SendRequest 实际字段 | 修正 |
622	|----------|---------------------|------|
623	| `stepConfig.sendConfig.fieldValues` | `userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, SendFieldValue>>` | 重命名 |
624	| `stepConfig.sendConfig.targetId ?? definition.targetId ?? ''` | `targetId: string`（必需） | 移除 fallback |
625	| `stepConfig.sendConfig.options ?? {}` | 不存在 | 移除 |
626	| 缺失 | `variables?: VariableMap` | 新增 |
627	
628	修正后：
629	
630	```typescript
631	function buildSendRequest(
632	  stepConfig: SendStepConfig,
633	  definition: TaskDefinition,
634	  stepIndex: number,
635	  iteration: number,
636	): SendRequest {
637	  return {
638	    frameId: stepConfig.frameId,
639	    targetId: stepConfig.targetId,
640	    userFieldValues: resolveFieldValues(
641	      stepConfig.userFieldValues,
642	      definition.fieldVariations,
643	      iteration,
644	    ),
645	    variables: stepConfig.variables,
646	    context: { source: 'task', taskId: definition.id, stepIndex },
647	  };
648	}
649	```
650	
651	### 4.2 wait-condition step 执行器
652	
653	从注册单个 `WaitCondition` 改为注册条件组。
654	
655	```typescript
656	async function executeWaitConditionStep(
657	  instanceId: string,
658	  step: WaitConditionStepDefinition,
659	  iteration: number,
660	  stepIndex: number,
661	  signal: Promise<void>,
662	): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; interrupted: boolean }> {
663	  const { conditions, timeoutMs, onTimeout } = step.config;
664	
665	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
666	    let settled = false;
667	
668	    const group = ctx.conditionRegistry.registerGroup(conditions, () => {
669	      if (settled) return;
670	      settled = true;
671	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
672	      if (timer !== undefined) clearTimeout(timer);
673	      resolve({
674	        result: { kind: 'wait-condition', stepIndex, iteration, matched: true, timedOut: false },
675	        interrupted: false,
676	      });
677	    });
678	
679	    const timer = timeoutMs !== undefined
680	      ? setTimeout(() => {
681	          if (settled) return;
682	          settled = true;
683	          ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
684	          resolve({
685	            result: { kind: 'wait-condition', stepIndex, iteration, matched: false, timedOut: true },
686	            interrupted: false,
687	          });
688	        }, timeoutMs)
689	      : undefined;
690	
691	    signal.then(() => {
692	      if (settled) return;
693	      settled = true;
694	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
695	      if (timer !== undefined) clearTimeout(timer);
696	      resolve({
697	        result: { kind: 'wait-condition', stepIndex, iteration, matched: false, timedOut: false },
698	        interrupted: true,
699	      });
700	    });
701	  });
702	}
703	```
704	
705	### 4.3 delay step 执行器
706	
707	无变更。现有 `executeDelayStep`（task-step-executors.ts:117-135）已满足需求。
708	
709	### 4.4 intervalAfterMs 处理
710	
711	在 `executeSteps` 循环中，send step 执行后检查 `intervalAfterMs`：
712	
713	```typescript
714	// 在 executeSteps 的 step 循环内：
715	if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.intervalAfterMs) {
716	  const delay = sleep(step.config.intervalAfterMs);
717	  const race = await Promise.race([delay.promise.then(() => 'done'), signal.then(() => 'intr')]);
718	  if (race === 'intr') { delay.cancel(); return false; }
719	}
720	```
721

724	## 5. TimerService adapter

726	### 5.1 接口定义

728	在 `adapters/ports.ts` 中新增：

730	```typescript
731	export interface TimerService {
732	  now(): number;
733	  setTimeout(callback: () => void, delayMs: number): number;
734	  clearTimeout(id: number): void;
735	  setInterval(callback: () => void, intervalMs: number): number;
736	  clearInterval(id: number): void;
737	}

742	```typescript
743	export interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {
744	  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider;
745	  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource;
746	  readonly timerService?: TimerService;       // 可选
747	  readonly fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;  // 新增

756	|------|------|

767	## 6. 状态管理
768	
769	### 6.1 TaskInstanceState
770	
771	无结构性变更。`definitionRef` 类型从旧 `TaskDefinition` 更新为新 `TaskDefinition`。
772	
773	### 6.2 stepResults 上限
774	
775	长期运行任务可能产生大量 stepResults。
776	
777	策略：保留最近 N 条（默认 100，可通过 `CreateTaskServiceOptions` 配置）。超出时丢弃最旧的结果。不影响进度计算（进度基于计数器而非 stepResults 数组长度）。
778	
779	### 6.3 进度计算
780	
781	`calculateProgress`（core/progress.ts）逻辑不变，适配新类型即可。`iterationsTotal` 从 `resolvedStop.maxIterations` 计算。

904	## 8. 翻译约定
905	
906	### 8.1 startDelay → delay step
907	
908	旧 `delayBeforeStartMs` → steps 首位插入 delay step。由 UI 或 SCOE adapter 在构造 TaskDefinition 时处理。
909	
910	### 8.2 responseDelay → delay step
911	
912	旧触发后 `responseDelay` → event schedule 的 steps 首位插入 delay step。
913	
914	### 8.3 SCOE 参数 resolve
915	
916	SCOE adapter 翻译时将参数值 resolve 为 literal：
917	- 参数值 → `SendStepConfig.userFieldValues`
918	- 完成条件阈值 → `ConditionTerm.threshold`
919	- task 引擎不理解参数关联
920	
921	### 8.4 文件发送
922	
923	SCOE adapter 读取文件 → `FieldVariation[]`：
924	- `.txt`：按行分割，过滤空行
925	- 非 `.txt`：逗号分隔
926	- `maxLength = max(values.length)` → 自动 `stopCondition.maxIterations`
927	
928	### 8.5 单帧发送
929	
930	```typescript
931	{
932	  id: generateId(),
933	  name: 'single-send',
934	  steps: [{ kind: 'send', id: 'step-0', config: { frameId, targetId, userFieldValues } }],
935	  schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },
936	  errorPolicy: { onFailure: 'stop' },
937	}
938	```
939

154	- 状态筛选 QChip 组: 全部/待启动/运行中/已暂停/已完成/失败
155	- 数据源: selectActiveInstances + selectTaskHistory
156	- **历史记录数据流**: task state 已有 `instances`（活跃）+ `history`（removeTask 归档）两层数据。task feature 新增 `selectActiveInstances` selector，只返回非终止态实例（created/running/paused）。`selectTaskHistory` 扩展为返回 history + 仍在 instances 中的终止态实例（completed/failed/stopped）。UI composable 直接调两个 selector，不做任何过滤。

179	- 高级配置（QExpansionItem 折叠）:
180	  - 可变参数: FieldVariation 编辑器（字段选择 + 值列表）
181	  - 停止条件: maxIterations / maxDurationMs / exitCondition
182	  - 错误策略: onFailure(QSelect) / retryCount / retryDelayMs
183	- 操作: [取消] [保存] [保存并启动]
184	  - [保存] `:loading="isSaving" :disable="!isValid"`(F4)
185	  - [保存并启动] `:loading="isSaving"`
186	- 保存前 validateTaskDefinition 校验

1	import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';
2	import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
3	
4	// --- Step kinds ---
5	
6	export const TASK_STEP_KINDS = ['send', 'wait-condition', 'delay'] as const;
7	export type TaskStepKind = (typeof TASK_STEP_KINDS)[number];
8	
9	// --- Comparison ---
10	
11	export const COMPARISON_OPERATORS = [
12	  'eq',
13	  'neq',
14	  'gt',
15	  'lt',
16	  'gte',
17	  'lte',
18	  'contains',
19	  'change',
20	  'any',
21	] as const;
22	export type ComparisonOperator = (typeof COMPARISON_OPERATORS)[number];
23	
24	// --- WaitCondition ---
25	
26	export interface WaitCondition {
27	  readonly frameId: string;
28	  readonly fieldId: string;
29	  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
30	  readonly threshold: number | string;
31	  readonly sourceId?: string;
32	}
33	
34	// --- Step definitions ---
35	
36	export interface SendStepConfig {
37	  readonly frameId: string;
38	  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;
39	  readonly targetId?: string;
40	  readonly options?: { readonly checksumKind?: string; readonly autoChecksum?: boolean };
41	}
42	
43	export interface WaitConditionStepConfig {
44	  readonly condition: WaitCondition;
45	  readonly timeoutMs: number;
46	  readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';
47	}
48	
49	export interface DelayStepConfig {
50	  readonly durationMs: number;
51	}
52	
53	export interface SendStepDefinition {
54	  readonly id: string;
55	  readonly name?: string;
56	  readonly kind: 'send';
57	  readonly sendConfig: SendStepConfig;
58	}
59	
60	export interface WaitConditionStepDefinition {
61	  readonly id: string;
62	  readonly name?: string;
63	  readonly kind: 'wait-condition';
64	  readonly waitConfig: WaitConditionStepConfig;
65	}
66	
67	export interface DelayStepDefinition {
68	  readonly id: string;
69	  readonly name?: string;
70	  readonly kind: 'delay';
71	  readonly delayConfig: DelayStepConfig;
72	}
73	
74	export type TaskStepDefinition =
75	  | SendStepDefinition
76	  | WaitConditionStepDefinition
77	  | DelayStepDefinition;
78	
79	// --- Trigger / Scheduling ---
80	
81	export const TASK_TRIGGER_SOURCES = [
82	  'user-ui',
83	  'timer',
84	  'receive-trigger',
85	  'scoe-command',
86	  'northbound-command',
87	] as const;
88	export type TaskTriggerSource = (typeof TASK_TRIGGER_SOURCES)[number];
89	
90	export const TASK_SCHEDULING_MODES = ['timed', 'trigger', 'sequence'] as const;
91	export type TaskSchedulingMode = (typeof TASK_SCHEDULING_MODES)[number];
92	
93	// --- Error policy ---
94	
95	export const TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS = ['retry', 'skip-step', 'stop', 'pause'] as const;
96	export type TaskErrorAction = (typeof TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS)[number];
97	
98	export interface TaskErrorPolicy {
99	  readonly onFailure: TaskErrorAction;
100	  readonly retryCount?: number;
101	  readonly retryDelayMs?: number;
102	}
103	
104	// --- Stop condition ---
105	
106	export interface TaskStopCondition {
107	  readonly maxDurationMs?: number;
108	  readonly maxIterations?: number;
109	}
110	
111	// --- TaskDefinition ---
112	
113	export interface TaskDefinition {
114	  readonly id: string;
115	  readonly name: string;
116	  readonly schedulingMode: TaskSchedulingMode;
117	  readonly triggerSource: TaskTriggerSource;
118	  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
119	  readonly targetId?: string;
120	  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
121	  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
122	  readonly intervalMs?: number;
123	  readonly delayBeforeStartMs?: number;
124	  readonly triggerCondition?: WaitCondition;
125	  readonly cooldownMs?: number;
126	}
127	
128	// --- Lifecycle ---
129	
130	export const TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES = [
131	  'created',
132	  'running',
133	  'paused',
134	  'stopped',
135	  'completed',
136	  'failed',
137	] as const;
138	export type TaskLifecycleStatus = (typeof TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES)[number];
139	
140	export type LifecycleAction = 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'stop' | 'complete' | 'fail';
141	
142	// --- Step results ---
143	
144	export interface SendStepResult {
145	  readonly kind: 'send';
146	  readonly sendResult: SendResult;
147	}
148	
149	export interface WaitConditionStepResult {
150	  readonly kind: 'wait-condition';
151	  readonly matched: boolean;
152	  readonly matchedValue?: number | string;
153	  readonly timedOut: boolean;
154	}
155	
156	export interface DelayStepResult {
157	  readonly kind: 'delay';
158	  readonly completed: boolean;
159	}
160	
161	export type TaskStepResultPayload =
162	  | SendStepResult
163	  | WaitConditionStepResult
164	  | DelayStepResult;
165	
166	export interface TaskStepResult extends TaskStepResultPayload {
167	  readonly stepIndex: number;
168	  readonly iteration: number;
169	  readonly appliedPolicy?: TaskErrorAction;
170	}
171	
172	// --- Instance state ---
173	
174	export interface TaskInstanceState {
175	  readonly instanceId: string;
176	  readonly definitionRef: TaskDefinition;
177	  readonly lifecycle: TaskLifecycleStatus;
178	  readonly startedAt?: string;
179	  readonly pausedAt?: string;
180	  readonly stoppedAt?: string;
181	  readonly completedAt?: string;
182	  readonly failedAt?: string;
183	  readonly currentStepIndex: number;
184	  readonly currentIteration: number;
185	  readonly stepResults: readonly TaskStepResult[];
186	  readonly error?: string;
187	}
188	
189	export type ReadonlyTaskInstanceState = ReadonlyDeep<TaskInstanceState>;
190	
191	// --- Progress ---
192	
193	export interface TaskProgress {
194	  readonly stepsTotal: number;
195	  readonly stepsCompleted: number;
196	  readonly stepsFailed: number;
197	  readonly stepsSkipped: number;
198	  readonly iterationsCompleted: number;
199	  readonly iterationsTotal: number | null;
200	  readonly elapsedMs: number;
201	  readonly estimatedRemainingMs: number | null;
202	  readonly lastStepResult: TaskStepResult | null;
203	}
204	
205	// --- Execution summary ---
206	
207	export interface TaskExecutionSummary {
208	  readonly kind: 'completed' | 'stopped' | 'failed';
209	  readonly summary: {
210	    readonly stepsTotal: number;
211	    readonly stepsSucceeded: number;
212	    readonly stepsFailed: number;
213	    readonly stepsSkipped: number;
214	  };
215	  readonly durationMs: number;
216	  readonly startedAt: string;
217	  readonly finishedAt: string;
218	}
219	
220	// --- Condition term (extends WaitCondition with logic operator) ---
221	
222	export interface ConditionTerm extends WaitCondition {
223	  readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or';
224	}
225	
226	// --- Condition match input ---
227	// Decoupled from receive internals. Service layer maps from receive public API outputs.
228	
229	export interface ConditionMatchInput {
230	  readonly frameId: string;
231	  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
232	  readonly sourceId?: string;
233	}
234

1	import type { WaitCondition, ConditionTerm, ConditionMatchInput } from '../core';
2	import { evaluateCondition, evaluateConditionGroup } from '../core';
3	
4	interface RegistryGroup {
5	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];
6	  readonly callback: (value?: number | string) => void;
7	  readonly singleMode?: boolean; // legacy register() mode
8	}
9	
10	export interface ConditionEntry {
11	  readonly _group: RegistryGroup;
12	}
13	
14	export interface ConditionGroup {
15	  readonly _group: RegistryGroup;
16	}
17	
18	export class ConditionRegistry {
19	  private groups: RegistryGroup[] = [];
20	  private frameIndex = new Map<string, Set<number>>();
21	
22	  register(condition: WaitCondition, onMatch: (value: number | string) => void): ConditionEntry {
23	    const term: ConditionTerm = { ...condition };
24	    const group: RegistryGroup = { conditions: [term], callback: onMatch, singleMode: true };
25	    const idx = this.groups.length;
26	    this.groups.push(group);
27	    this.indexGroup(idx, group);
28	    return { _group: group };
29	  }
30	
31	  unregister(entry: ConditionEntry): void {
32	    const idx = this.groups.indexOf(entry._group);
33	    if (idx === -1) return;
34	    this.groups.splice(idx, 1);
35	    this.rebuildIndex();
36	  }
37	
38	  registerGroup(conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[], callback: () => void): ConditionGroup {
39	    const group: RegistryGroup = { conditions, callback: () => callback() };
40	    const idx = this.groups.length;
41	    this.groups.push(group);
42	    this.indexGroup(idx, group);
43	    return { _group: group };
44	  }
45	
46	  unregisterGroup(group: ConditionGroup): void {
47	    const idx = this.groups.indexOf(group._group);
48	    if (idx === -1) return;
49	    this.groups.splice(idx, 1);
50	    this.rebuildIndex();
51	  }
52	
53	  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
54	    const candidateIndices = this.frameIndex.get(input.frameId);
55	    if (!candidateIndices) return;
56	
57	    for (const idx of candidateIndices) {
58	      const group = this.groups[idx];
59	      if (!group) continue;
60	
61	      // Check sourceId filter
62	      const hasSourceFilter = group.conditions.some((c) => c.sourceId !== undefined);
63	      if (hasSourceFilter && input.sourceId !== undefined) {
64	        const anyMatch = group.conditions.some(
65	          (c) => c.sourceId === undefined || c.sourceId === input.sourceId,
66	        );
67	        if (!anyMatch) continue;
68	      }
69	
70	      if (group.singleMode) {
71	        const cond = group.conditions[0]!;
72	        if (evaluateCondition(cond, input)) {
73	          const value = input.fieldValues[cond.fieldId];
74	          if (value != null) {
75	            group.callback(value);
76	          }
77	        }
78	      } else {
79	        if (evaluateConditionGroup(group.conditions, { ...input.fieldValues })) {
80	          group.callback();
81	        }
82	      }
83	    }
84	  }
85	
86	  clear(): void {
87	    this.groups = [];
88	    this.frameIndex.clear();
89	  }
90	
91	  get size(): number {
92	    return this.groups.length;
93	  }
94	
95	  private indexGroup(idx: number, group: RegistryGroup): void {
96	    for (const cond of group.conditions) {
97	      if (!this.frameIndex.has(cond.frameId)) {
98	        this.frameIndex.set(cond.frameId, new Set());
99	      }
100	      this.frameIndex.get(cond.frameId)!.add(idx);
101	    }
102	  }
103	
104	  private rebuildIndex(): void {
105	    this.frameIndex.clear();
106	    for (let i = 0; i < this.groups.length; i++) {
107	      this.indexGroup(i, this.groups[i]!);
108	    }
109	  }
110	}
111

1	import type { WaitCondition, ConditionTerm, ConditionMatchInput } from './types';
2	import { compareValues } from '@/shared/condition-operators';
3	
4	export function evaluateCondition(
5	  condition: WaitCondition,
6	  input: ConditionMatchInput,
7	): boolean {
8	  if (input.frameId !== condition.frameId) {
9	    return false;
10	  }
11	  if (condition.sourceId !== undefined) {
12	    if (input.sourceId === undefined || condition.sourceId !== input.sourceId) {
13	      return false;
14	    }
15	  }
16	  const value = input.fieldValues[condition.fieldId];
17	  if (value === undefined) return false;
18	  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);
19	}
20	
21	export function evaluateSingleCondition(
22	  condition: ConditionTerm,
23	  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,
24	): boolean {
25	  const value = fieldValues[condition.fieldId];
26	  if (value === null || value === undefined) return false;
27	  return compareValues(value, condition.threshold, condition.operator);
28	}
29	
30	export function evaluateConditionGroup(
31	  conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[],
32	  fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null>,
33	): boolean {
34	  if (conditions.length === 0) return true;
35	
36	  let result = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[0]!, fieldValues);
37	
38	  for (let i = 1; i < conditions.length; i++) {
39	    const matches = evaluateSingleCondition(conditions[i]!, fieldValues);
40	    const logicOp = conditions[i]!.logicOperator ?? 'and';
41	
42	    if (logicOp === 'or') {
43	      result = result || matches;
44	    } else {
45	      result = result && matches;
46	    }
47	  }
48	  return result;
49	}
50
